PASS_REAL_AUTH_SESSION_RUNTIME_ADAPTER_IMPLEMENTATION_DESIGN_ONLY

Next step decision
P4.10.0-C8-I-D4-L-L-O-BC - Controlled Activity Intake Real Auth Session Runtime Adapter Static Implementation

Reason for O-BB
O-AZ created the real auth session runtime read contract.
O-BA-FIX1 verified that contract.
O-BB inspects the current project Auth0/session integration with git grep only and decides the safe implementation boundary for the future runtime adapter.
O-BB does not implement the adapter and does not execute any real Auth0/session runtime read.

Current verified state
- serverSideAuthSession.ts exists and is static/no-I/O.
- serverSideAuthSessionRuntime.ts does not exist yet.
- route.ts does not call serverSideAuthSession.ts or a runtime adapter.
- route.ts still supports no-write preview modes only.
- productionWriteEnabled=false remains in route.ts.
- no Supabase read resolver is approved.
- no activity_events insert is approved.

Allowed in O-BC
O-BC may create only:
- lib/activity/controlledIntake/serverSideAuthSessionRuntime.ts
- docs/activity/p4-10-0-c8-i-d4-l-l-o-bc-real-auth-session-runtime-adapter-static-implementation.txt

O-BC may not modify:
- src/app/api/activity/controlled-intake/route.ts
- lib/activity/controlledIntake/serverSideAuthSession.ts
- lib/activity/controlledIntake/serverSideAppUserMapping.ts
- lib/activity/controlledIntake/authContext.ts
- lib/activity/controlledIntake/productionAuthMapping.ts
- lib/activity/controlledIntake/persist.ts
- database schema
- migrations

O-BC helper type
O-BC should create the runtime adapter file, but it must be implementation-safe and not called by route.ts.
The first O-BC implementation should be a server-only adapter boundary with an explicit approval switch.
It may define the future shape and controlled failure behavior.
It should not execute real Auth0/session reads during tests.

Required O-BC design choice
Because O-BB is only a design gate, O-BC should use a controlled adapter pattern:
- default behavior without explicit approval must fail with CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_RUNTIME_NOT_APPROVED
- runtime read execution must be guarded behind allowRuntimeRead=true
- actual Auth0/project session read should remain isolated in a tiny internal function
- tests should verify fail-closed paths without calling real Auth0/session
- no route integration is allowed in O-BC

Recommended O-BC imports
O-BC may import types and builder from:
- lib/activity/controlledIntake/serverSideAuthSession.ts

O-BC should not import:
- route.ts
- Supabase clients
- OpenAI clients
- DB helpers
- semantic/category/value/state helpers

Auth0 import decision
O-BB does not authorize direct project-wide assumptions beyond the collected evidence.
O-BC may include a runtime adapter skeleton that is ready to connect to the project-approved Auth0 helper later, but the actual call must remain protected by explicit approval and must not be executed by the O-BC tests.
If the current project has a stable server-side Auth0 helper, a later O-BD/O-BE gate may connect the adapter to it.
If the helper boundary is ambiguous, O-BC should stop at adapter skeleton and not import Auth0 SDK.

Required O-BC exports
- CONTROLLED_ACTIVITY_INTAKE_SERVER_SIDE_AUTH_SESSION_RUNTIME_LAYER
- ControlledActivityIntakeServerSideAuthSessionRuntimeSource
- ControlledActivityIntakeServerSideAuthSessionRuntimeInput
- ControlledActivityIntakeServerSideAuthSessionRuntimeReadModel
- ControlledActivityIntakeServerSideAuthSessionRuntimeGuardrails
- ControlledActivityIntakeServerSideAuthSessionRuntimeFailureCode
- ControlledActivityIntakeServerSideAuthSessionRuntimeSuccess
- ControlledActivityIntakeServerSideAuthSessionRuntimeFailure
- ControlledActivityIntakeServerSideAuthSessionRuntimeResult
- readControlledActivityIntakeServerSideAuthSessionRuntime
- isControlledActivityIntakeServerSideAuthSessionRuntimeFailure

Required O-BC failure codes
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_RUNTIME_NOT_APPROVED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_SESSION_RUNTIME_UNAVAILABLE
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_SERVER_SESSION_REQUIRED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_SUBJECT_REQUIRED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_SESSION_INVALID
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_PROVIDER_UNSUPPORTED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_SESSION_READ_FAILED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_SESSION_EXPIRED

Required O-BC fail-closed checks
- allowRuntimeRead missing -> CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_RUNTIME_NOT_APPROVED
- allowRuntimeRead=false -> CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_RUNTIME_NOT_APPROVED
- auth boundary missing/unsupported -> CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_SESSION_RUNTIME_UNAVAILABLE
- injected runtime read error -> CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_SESSION_READ_FAILED
- missing session -> CONTROLLED_INTAKE_SERVER_SESSION_REQUIRED
- missing sub/authSubject -> CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_SUBJECT_REQUIRED
- expired session -> CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_SESSION_EXPIRED
- malformed session -> CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_SESSION_INVALID

Required O-BC success checks without real Auth0 runtime
For O-BC tests, success may be verified only with a strictly injected sanitized runtime read model, not real Auth0/session.
The test may confirm conversion into the static helper input/output shape.
The test must not call real cookies/headers/session.

Required O-BC guardrails
- runtimeHelperOnly=true
- auth0RuntimeReadExecuted=false in O-BC tests
- serverSideSessionReadOnly=true
- serverSideContextRequired=true
- clientOwnershipAccepted=false
- previewHeaderAcceptedForProductionWrite=false
- dbReadExecuted=false
- dbWriteExecuted=false
- sqlExecuted=false
- aiCallExecuted=false
- semanticCandidatesPersisted=false
- valueObjectsCreated=false
- stateFactsCreated=false
- stateDeltasCreated=false
- stateSnapshotsCreated=false

Forbidden O-BC behavior
- route.ts integration
- production write mode
- activity_events insert
- Supabase read
- Supabase write
- SQL execution
- AI call
- semantic/category derivation
- value object creation
- state facts/deltas/snapshots
- trusting client-provided appUserId/user_id/actorId/organizationId/spaceId
- leaking tokens or raw session

Forbidden O-BC output
The adapter must not output:
- access token
- refresh token
- ID token
- raw cookie
- raw session object
- full Auth0 profile
- full provider payload
- name/picture unless a later explicit gate approves it

Required O-BC verification
- npx tsc --noEmit must pass.
- isolated runtime adapter check must pass.
- route.ts must not change.
- serverSideAuthSession.ts must not change.
- serverSideAppUserMapping.ts must not change.
- authContext.ts must not change.
- productionAuthMapping.ts must not change.
- persist.ts must not change.
- only serverSideAuthSessionRuntime.ts and O-BC report may change.
- no DB/SQL/AI markers may appear.
- real Auth0/session runtime read must not be executed during O-BC.

Local evidence collection note
O-BB collected local Auth0/session implementation evidence with git grep only.
This is not an Auth0 runtime call.
This is not a session read.
This is not a Supabase read.
This is not SQL execution.
This is not a DB write.

Evidence count
- Local Auth0/session implementation evidence count: 732

Important limitation after O-BB
O-BB approves only the implementation design for a runtime adapter file.
It does not approve route integration.
It does not approve production write.
It does not approve Supabase app-user mapping read.
It does not approve activity_events insert.
It does not approve AI or semantic persistence.

Future corridor after O-BC
After O-BC implementation and O-BD verification:
- decide whether to connect the adapter to the actual project Auth0 helper
- verify real server-side session read in a no-write route-safe way
- design Supabase app-user mapping read resolver
- only later integrate route
- only much later enable controlled production writes

Still forbidden after O-BB
- Do not integrate runtime adapter into route.ts.
- Do not execute Auth0/session runtime reads from route.ts.
- Do not execute DB reads.
- Do not execute DB writes.
- Do not execute SQL.

- Do not call AI.

- Do not persist category candidates.
- Do not create Value Objects.
- Do not write state facts, deltas, or snapshots.
